# Feat candidate casebook

Source-authored exact candidate; separate exact review pending. Selection/runtime disabled. Original catalogs unchanged.

The following are complete authored expectations, not executed full feat-runtime tests. Original examples and their final outcomes are retained at every original position.

```json
  "basis": "Restore persistent ordinary caltrop hazard and physical disarm; retain finite prepared snare/charge, setup budgets and source-scoped reaction movement without remote deletion or recycled hard denial.",
  "assessmentKind": "Authored source-based semantic adjudication; not executed feat runtime or campaign authentication"
}
```
